-- [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicateCompany] | References permission/temp-filter machinery



/*RZ980516*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispCheckDuplicateCompany]
(		
/* 
	EXEC ApispCheckDuplicateCompany 1, 0, 'test', 1, 1, 3, N'lack', 1033, 1065
*/
@Type INT, 
@Id INT, 
@Val NVARCHAR(50), 
@TitleID INT, 
@CityId INT, 
@UserID INT,
@LackOfShowText NVARCHAR(50) = NULL,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)

AS
BEGIN
/**/
IF(@TitleID IS NULL)
	SET @TitleID = 0
/**/
IF(@LackOfShowText IS NULL OR @LackOfShowText = '')
	SET @LackOfShowText = N'عدم نمايش'

DECLARE @fld VARCHAR(MAX), @fldLimit VARCHAR(MAX), @from VARCHAR(MAX), @term VARCHAR(MAX)
/*--*/
EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID
DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(4000)
SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
WHERE TypeId = 20
AND UserId = @UserId
/*--*/
/*--*/
DECLARE @Word NVARCHAR(50)
IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
BEGIN
	SET @fld = 
		' Select DISTINCT TOP 100 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, cmpName, 
		(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId) Title,
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL(cmpName, '''') CompanyWithTitle,
		cmpTel, cmpTelII, cmpFax, 
		(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country WHERE CountryId = cmpCountryId) Country,
		(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = cmpProvinceId) Provice,
		(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = cmpCityId) City,
		ISNULL( (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = cmpProvinceId), '''') + '' '' +
		ISNULL( (SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = cmpCityId), '''') + '' '' +
		ISNULL(cmpAddress, '''') cmpAddress,
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersontitleId = prsTitleId), '''')+ '' '' +
		ISNULL(prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ISNULL(prsFName, '''') prsName, 
		prsTel, prsTelII, prsFax, prsMobile, prsEmail, prsEmailII, cmpNumber,
		(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''')
		FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId) ReferUser '
		/*--------------------------------*/
	/*DECLARE @Word NVARCHAR(50)*/
	SET @Word = @LackOfShowText
	SET @fldLimit = 
		' Select DISTINCT TOP 100 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, cmpName, 
		(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId) Title,
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL(cmpName, '''') CompanyWithTitle,
		'''+@Word+''' cmpTel, 
		'''+@Word+''' cmpTelII, 
		'''+@Word+''' cmpFax, 
		(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country WHERE CountryId = cmpCountryId) Country,
		(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = cmpProvinceId) Provice,
		(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = cmpCityId) City,
		'''+@Word+''' cmpAddress,
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle WHERE PersontitleId = prsTitleId), '''')+ '' '' +
		ISNULL(prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ISNULL(prsFName, '''') prsName, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsTel, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsTelII, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsFax, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsMobile, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsEmail, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsEmailII, 
		cmpNumber,
		(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''')
		FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId) ReferUser '
	/*--------------------------------*/						
	IF @Type = 0  /*For Search Likely*/
	BEGIN
		IF @Id > 0 /*Edit Mode*/
		BEGIN
		SET @from = 
				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId '
		SET @term= 
				' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
							NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') ) 
				  And CompanyID <> '+Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
		END
		ELSE IF @id = 0 /*Insert Mode*/
		BEGIN 
		SET @from =
				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 
				' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
							NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+ 
							REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
							NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.prsLName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') )' 
			end
		END 
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID <> 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN 
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			 ') AND cmpTitleId = ' + Cast(@TitleID AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID = 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(cmpName AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ 
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			  /*AND cmpTitleId = @TitleID*/
			') AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
/*--*/
END
ELSE
BEGIN
	SET @fld = 
		' Select DISTINCT TOP 100 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, 
		(SELECT CLN.cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') cmpName, 
		(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') Title,
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT CLN.cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') CompanyWithTitle,
		cmpTel, cmpTelII, cmpFax, 
		(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country_Lang CY WHERE CY.CountryId = cmpCountryId AND CY.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') Country,
		(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PR WHERE PR.ProvinceId = cmpProvinceId AND PR.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') Provice,
		(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CI WHERE CI.CityId = cmpCityId AND CI.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') City,
		ISNULL( (SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PR WHERE PR.ProvinceId = cmpProvinceId AND PR.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') + '' '' +
		ISNULL( (SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang CI WHERE CI.CityId = cmpCityId AND CI.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') + '' '' +
		ISNULL((SELECT CLN.cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') , '''') cmpAddress,
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PT WHERE PT.PersontitleId = prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''')+ '' '' +
		ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(PLN.prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ISNULL(PLN.prsFName, '''') FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND PLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') prsName, 
		prsTel, prsTelII, prsFax, prsMobile, prsEmail, prsEmailII, cmpNumber,
		(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''')
		FROM dbo.Users_Lang ULN WHERE ULN.UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId AND ULN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') ReferUser '
	/*--------------------------------*/	
	SET @Word = @LackOfShowText
	SET @fldLimit = 
		' Select DISTINCT TOP 100 CompanyID, CompanyID cmpId, 
		(SELECT CLN.cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') cmpName, 
		(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') Title,
		ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND Ct.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') + 
		'' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT CLN.cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''') CompanyWithTitle,
		'''+@Word+''' cmpTel, 
		'''+@Word+''' cmpTelII, 
		'''+@Word+''' cmpFax, 
		(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country_Lang CY WHERE CY.CountryId = cmpCountryId AND CY.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') Country,
		(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang PR WHERE PR.ProvinceId = cmpProvinceId AND PR.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') Provice,
		(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_LAng CI WHERE CI.CityId = cmpCityId AND CI.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') City,
		'''+@Word+''' cmpAddress,
		ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PT WHERE PT.PersontitleId = prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), '''')+ '' '' +
		ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ISNULL(prsFName, '''') FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang PLN WHERE PLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND PLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '),'''') prsName, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsTel, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsTelII, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsFax, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsMobile, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsEmail, 
		'''+@Word+''' prsEmailII, 
		cmpNumber,
		(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''')
		FROM dbo.Users_Lang ULN WHERE ULN.UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId AND ULN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + ') ReferUser '
	/*--------------------------------*/						
	IF @Type = 0  /*For Search Likely*/
	BEGIN
		IF @Id > 0 /*Edit Mode*/
		BEGIN
		SET @from = 
				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId '
		SET @term= 
				' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
							NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
					+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') ) 
				  And CompanyID <> '+Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
		END
		ELSE IF @id = 0 /*Insert Mode*/
		BEGIN 
		SET @from =
				' From CRMCompany C LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 
				' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
							NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+ 
							REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
							NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'')  
				OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR prsFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsMobile = '''+@Val+
				''' OR prsEmail = '''+@Val+''' OR prsEmailII = '''+@Val+''' 
				OR REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID  AND CPLN.LanguageID = ' 
					+ CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), CP.prsLName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) Like (''%'+
				REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) +'%'') )' 
			end
		END 
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID <> 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN 
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
						NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			 ') AND cmpTitleId = ' + Cast(@TitleID AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			 ' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
	ELSE IF (@Type = 1  AND @TitleID = 0)  /*For Search "=" (before post)*/
	BEGIN
		SET @from =
			' From CRMCompany C  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CP ON C.CompanyID = CP.prsCompanyId'
		SET @term = 	
			' Where ( REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang CLN WHERE CLN.CompanyID = C.CompanyID  AND CLN.LanguageID = ' + CAST(@LanguageID AS NVARCHAR(MAX)) + '), cmpName) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
						NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) = '''+ 
						REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(@Val AS NVARCHAR(2000)), NCHAR(1740),
					NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)) + '''  
			OR cmpTel = '''+@Val+
				''' OR cmpTelII = '''+@Val+''' OR cmpFax = '''+@Val+''' OR prsTel = '''+@Val+ ''''+
			  /*AND cmpTitleId = @TitleID*/
			') AND cmpCityId = ' + Cast(@CityId AS VARCHAR(50))+
			' And CompanyID <> ' + Cast(@Id AS VARCHAR(50))
	END
END

/*--*/
/*--*/
/*--*/
IF @cmpDataPermision <> ''
BEGIN
EXECUTE(
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fld+ @from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +
' ) C union ' + 
' SELECT * FROM ( ' +
@fldLimit+ @from+ @term +
') d
where CmpId Not IN ( SELECT CompanyId '+
@from+ @term +@cmpDataPermision +' )'
)
END
ELSE
EXECUTE(@fld+ @from+ @term)
/**/
END





GO
-- [dbo].[ApispCompanyMultiSelect] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*RZ14000427*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispCompanyMultiSelect]
/* 
	EXEC ApispCompanyMultiSelect
	@FieldName = N'PhoneAndMob',
	@FieldValue = N'5555',
	@UserId = 54, 
	@LanguageID = 1033,
	@DefaultLanguageID = 1065
*/
(
@FieldName NVARCHAR(50),
@FieldValue NVARCHAR(200),
@UserId INT = NULL, /*UserId is null or 0 ==> show all company*/
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	/*=====================================================*/
	DECLARE @clmn VARCHAR(MAX) = N'', @Term VARCHAR(MAX) = N'', @From VARCHAR(MAX) = N''
	DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(MAX) = N''
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	SET @Term = N' WHERE 1 = 1 '
	SET @From = N' FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany] C '
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	IF(@FieldName = N'CmpMob')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Term = @Term + N' AND C.[cmpMobile] = ''' + @FieldValue  + ''''
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@FieldName = N'CmpEmail')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Term = @Term + N' AND C.[cmpEmail] = ''' + @FieldValue  + ''''
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@FieldName = N'CmpNumber')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Term = @Term + N' AND C.[cmpNumber] =' + @FieldValue 
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@FieldName = N'CmpName')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + N' AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(C.[CmpName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
							   NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) = ' + 
							   '(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @FieldValue + N''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))'
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF (@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + N' AND (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 CLN.[cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.[CompanyID] = C.[CompanyID] AND CLN.[LanguageID] =  ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + '),'''') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
							   NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) = ' + 
							   '(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @FieldValue + N''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
								NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))'
		/**/
		END							    
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@FieldName = N'PrsLName')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + N' AND C.[CompanyID] IN (SELECT [prsCompanyId]            
													  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP 
													  WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(CP.[prsLName] AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
															   NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) = ' + 
															   '(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @FieldValue + N''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
																NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))'+ ') '
		
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF (@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SET @Term = @Term + N' AND C.[CompanyID] IN (SELECT [prsCompanyId]            
													  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP 
													  WHERE (REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST((ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID AND CPLN.LanguageID =  ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + '),'''')) AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
															   NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740))) = ' + 
															   '(REPLACE(REPLACE(REPLACE(CAST(''' + @FieldValue + N''' AS NVARCHAR(2000)) ,
																NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)),NCHAR(1603),NCHAR(1705)),NCHAR(1609),NCHAR(1740)))'+ ') '
		
		/**/
		END							    
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@FieldName = N'PrsNationalCode')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Term = @Term + N' AND C.[CompanyID] IN (SELECT [prsCompanyId]            
													  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP 
													  WHERE CP.[prsNationalCode] = ''' + @FieldValue  + ''') '
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@FieldName = N'PrsMob')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Term = @Term + N' AND C.[CompanyID] IN (SELECT [prsCompanyId]            
													  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP 
													  WHERE CP.[prsMobile] = ''' + @FieldValue  + ''') '
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@FieldName = N'PrsEmail')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Term = @Term + N' AND C.[CompanyID] IN (SELECT [prsCompanyId]            
													  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP 
													  WHERE CP.[prsEmail] = ''' + @FieldValue  + '''' +
													  ' OR CP.[prsEmailII] = ''' + @FieldValue  + ''') '
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@FieldName = N'Email')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Term = @Term + N' AND (C.[cmpEmail] =''' + @FieldValue  + ''''
		SET @Term = @Term + N' OR C.[CompanyID] IN (SELECT [prsCompanyId]            
													  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP 
													  WHERE CP.[prsEmail] = ''' + @FieldValue  + '''' +
													  ' OR CP.[prsEmailII] = ''' + @FieldValue  + ''')) '
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(@FieldName = N'Phone')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @From = @From + N'  LEFT JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons CCP ON C.CompanyId = CCP.prsCompanyId  '

		SET @Term = @Term + N'  AND ( 
			LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) =  ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	 		
 			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '''')))+ LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) =  ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	  	 	
 			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '''') )) , 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel))+LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel )) =   ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	 	 	
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) =  ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	  			 	
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, '''')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) =  ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	  
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTel))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) ) =  ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	  
			
			OR ( ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	  BETWEEN 
				LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) AND 
				( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel))  )), 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  C.cmpTel)) )))-
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
						LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
						LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )) )) 
			AND 
			( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM(   ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	 )), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM(   ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	 )))-
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
					'''' ))	
			 = 
			( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) )), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTel, ''''))) + 
					LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTel)) )))-
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto )))+1, 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUpto ))), 
					'''' ))) 										
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) =  ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	  		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, '''')))+ LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) =  ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	  
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTelII)) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII )) =   ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	 		
			OR STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) =  ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	  		
			OR LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, '''')))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) =  ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	 			 	 
			OR SUBSTRING(LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''''))), 2, LEN(C.cmpPreTelII))+ 
					STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)), LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))), 
					LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) ) =  ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	  
			
			OR ( ''' + @FieldValue + N''' 	  BETWEEN
					LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII)) AND 
					( STUFF( LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelII))  )), 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( LTRIM( RTRIM( ISNULL(C.cmpPreTelII, ''''))) + LTRIM( RTRIM(  C.cmpTelII)) )))-
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )))+1, 
							LEN(LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII ))), 
							LTRIM( RTRIM( C.cmpTelUptoII )) )) 
				AND

			,[cmpCoordinateLastUserId] AS [ApiCmpCoordinateLastUserId]
			,(SELECT ISNULL(LName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName,'''') FROM dbo.Users U WHERE U.UserId = C.cmpCoordinateLastUserId) AS [ApiCmpCoordinateLastUser]
			,[cmpFOAccessLinkedCompany] AS [ApiCmpFOAccessLinkedCompany]
			,[cmpLinkAccCodeId] AS [ApiCmpLinkAccCodeId]

			,[cmpLinkAccCustomerId] AS [ApiCmpLinkAccCustomerId]
			,[cmpLinkAccCustomerIdStr] AS [ApiCmpLinkAccCustomerIdStr]
			,[cmpIsDraft] AS [ApiCmpIsDraft]

			,[cmpMainPersonId] AS [ApiCmpMainPersonId]
			,(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
					 ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
			  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP

			 ) AS [ApiCmpMainPerson]
			,(ISNULL((SELECT CT.CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId),'''') + '' '' +
				ISNULL([cmpName],''''))AS [ApiCompanyName]
		
		'
		/*------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF (@LanguageID IS NOT NULL AND @LanguageID <> @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
	/**/

		SET @clmn = N'SELECT

			,(SELECT CG.GroupIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIIID = C.cmpGroupIdII AND CG.LanguageID =  ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS [ApiCmpGroupII]
			,[cmpGroupIdIII] AS [ApiCmpGroupIdIII]
			,(SELECT CG.GroupIIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsIII_Lang CG WHERE CG.CompanyGroupIIIID = C.cmpGroupIdIII AND CG.LanguageID =  ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS [ApiCmpGroupIII]
			,[cmpGroupIdIV] AS [ApiCmpGroupIdIV]

			,[cmpTypeId] AS [ApiCmpTypeId]
			,(SELECT CT.CompanyTypeName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyType_Lang CT WHERE CT.CompanyTypeID = C.cmpTypeId AND CT.LanguageID =  ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS [ApiCmpType]

			,(SELECT ISNULL(LName , '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(FName,'''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang U WHERE U.UserId = C.cmpCoordinateLastUserId AND U.LanguageID =  ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS [ApiCmpCoordinateLastUser]
			,[cmpFOAccessLinkedCompany] AS [ApiCmpFOAccessLinkedCompany]
			,[cmpLinkAccCodeId] AS [ApiCmpLinkAccCodeId]
			,[cmpLinkAccCode] AS [ApiCmpLinkAccCode]
			,[cmpLinkAccCustomerId] AS [ApiCmpLinkAccCustomerId]
			,[cmpLinkAccCustomerIdStr] AS [ApiCmpLinkAccCustomerIdStr]
			,[cmpIsDraft] AS [ApiCmpIsDraft]
			,[cmpKind] AS [ApiCmpKind]
			,[cmpMainPersonId] AS [ApiCmpMainPersonId]
			,(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle_Lang] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CP.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID =  ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + '),'''')  + '' '' +
					 ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(CPLN.prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(CPLN.prsLName,'''') FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons_Lang] CPLN WHERE CPLN.CompanyPersonID = CP.CompanyPersonID AND CPLN.LanguageID =  ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + '),'''')
			  FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CP
			  WHERE CP.CompanyPersonID = [cmpMainPersonId]		
			 ) AS [ApiCmpMainPerson]
			,(ISNULL((SELECT CT.CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle_Lang CT WHERE CT.CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId AND CT.LanguageID =  ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + '),'''') + '' '' +
				ISNULL((SELECT TOP 1 [cmpName] FROM [dbo].[CrmCompany_Lang] CLN WHERE CLN.[CompanyID] = C.[CompanyID] AND CLN.[LanguageID] =  ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + '),''''))AS [ApiCompanyName]
		
		'
		/*------------------------------------------------*/
	/**/	
	END
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	/*-----------------------------------------------------------*/
	IF(ISNULL(@UserId,0) <> 0)
	BEGIN
	/**/
		EXEC dbo.CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserId
		/**/
		SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
		FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
		WHERE TypeId = 20
		AND UserId = @UserId
		/*--*/
		EXECUTE ( @clmn + @from + @Term + @cmpDataPermision + ' ORDER BY ApiCompanyId DESC, ApiCmpTitle ')
		/*--*/
		DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
		WHERE TypeId = 10
		AND UserId = @UserId
		/*--*/
		INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (UserId,	TXT, TypeId)
			 VALUES ( @UserId, ' SELECT CompanyId ' + @from + @Term, 10 )

	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(ISNULL(@UserId,0) =  0) 
	BEGIN
	/**/
		EXECUTE ( @clmn + @From + @Term + ' ORDER BY ApiCompanyId DESC, ApiCmpTitle')
	/**/
	END
	
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDeleteBuyOrder] | References permission/temp-filter machinery







/*NKR14030306*/
CREATE PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispDeleteBuyOrder]
(		
	/*
		EXEC [ApispDeleteBuyOrder] 17193 
	*/
	@BuyOrderId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	DECLARE @Res INT = 0

	BEGIN TRY
		/**/
		BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			/**/
			DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
			SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 bordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSBuyOrder WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId)
			IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) <> 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				EXEC ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart @BuyOrderId, 'bord'

				DELETE WSBuyOrder_Lang  WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId
				DELETE WSBuyOrder  WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId

				/*Check for delete*/
				DECLARE @FindRecordId INT
				SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 BuyOrderID FROM WSBuyOrder  WHERE BuyOrderID = @BuyOrderId)

				IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL) /*delete*/
					SET @Res = 1
				ELSE /*not delete*/
					SET @Res = 0
			/**/
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SET @Res = -2
			END
			/**/
		COMMIT
		/**/
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		/**/
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK
		SET @Res = 0
		/**/
	END CATCH
	/**/

	SELECT @Res AS Result

	/**/
END

			DELETE CrmCompany  WHERE CompanyID = @CompanyID
			SELECT 1 AS Result 

		COMMIT
	END TRY

			ROLLBACK		
		SELECT 0 AS Result
	END CATCH
/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDeleteDoc] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*NKR990514*/
Create Procedure [dbo].[ApispDeleteDoc]
(		
	/*
		exec ApispDeleteDoc 293
	*/
	@DocID INT
)
As
BEGIN
/**/
BEGIN TRY
    BEGIN TRANSACTION 
		/*RZ990516- Check isFixed*/
		DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
		SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 IsFix FROM dbo.WSDoc WHERE DocID = @DocID)
		IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) <> 1)
		BEGIN
		/**/
			DECLARE @Part NVARCHAR(200)='', @OprTypeId INT=0, @CanDelete BIT=0

			/* FIND OprType WITH @DocID */
			SELECT @OprTypeId = ISNULL(DO.OprType, 0) FROM WSDocOpr DO WHERE (SELECT D.OprId FROM WSDoc D WHERE D.DocID = @DocID) = DO.DocOprID


			IF (@OprTypeId = 1)
				SET @Part = 'WhDoc'
			ELSE IF (@OprTypeId = 2)
				SET @Part = 'WhDraft'
			ELSE IF (@OprTypeId = 3)
				SET @Part = 'WhMovement'
			ELSE
				SET @Part = ''
		
			IF (ISNULL(@Part, '') <> '')
			BEGIN

				EXEC ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart @DocID, @Part

				DELETE [dbo].[WSDoc_Lang]  WHERE DocID = @DocID
				DELETE [dbo].[WSDoc]  WHERE DocID = @DocID
				SELECT 1 AS Result

			END
			ELSE			
				SELECT 0 AS Result
		/**/
		END
		ELSE 
		BEGIN
			SELECT -2 AS Result
		END
		/*RZ990516- End*/
    COMMIT
END TRY
BEGIN CATCH
    IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
        ROLLBACK
	SELECT 0 AS Result
END CATCH
/**/
END





GO

/*NKR990513*/
/*RZ990515- Modify this sp*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispDeleteOrder]
(		
/*
	EXEC ApispDeleteOrder 17193 
*/
	@OrderId INT
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	/*RZ990515- Modify this sp*/
	BEGIN TRY
		BEGIN TRANSACTION 
			/*RZ990515- Check isFixed*/
			DECLARE @IsFixed BIT
			SET @IsFixed = (SELECT TOP 1 ordIsFixed FROM dbo.WSOrder WHERE OrderID = @OrderID)
			IF(ISNULL(@IsFixed,0) <> 1)
			BEGIN
			/**/
				EXEC ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart @OrderID, 'Ord'

				DELETE WSOrder_Lang  WHERE OrderID = @OrderID
					DELETE WSOrder  WHERE OrderID = @OrderID

				/*Check for delete*/
				DECLARE @FindRecordId INT
				SET @FindRecordId = (SELECT TOP 1 OrderID FROM WSOrder  WHERE OrderID = @OrderID)
				IF(@FindRecordId IS NULL) /*delete*/
					SELECT 1 as Result 
				ELSE
					SELECT 0 as Result /*not delete*/
			/**/
			END
			ELSE
			BEGIN
				SELECT -2 as Result
			END
			/*RZ990515- End*/
		COMMIT
	END TRY
	BEGIN CATCH
		IF @@TRANCOUNT > 0
			ROLLBACK
		SELECT 0 as Result
	END CATCH
	/**/
END

-- [dbo].[ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart] | Exact target






/*NKR990509 - CREATE FROM CRMspDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart*/
CREATE Procedure [dbo].[ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart]
(		
	/*
		exec ApispDyDeleteFormFieldMasterValuesBySysPart 6875,N'Cmp'
	*/
	@ObjectId INT,
	@SysPart NVARCHAR(50)
)
AS
BEGIN
/**/
	DECLARE @Qry NVARCHAR(MAX)='' , @Trem NVARCHAR(MAX)
	IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'cmp')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [CompanyId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'prs')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [CompanyPersonId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'prj')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [ProjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'cnvs')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [ConversationId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'pord')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'ord')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'bord')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'ssec')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'rsec')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whdoc')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whdraft')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'whmovement')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'prd')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	ELSE IF(LOWER(@SysPart) = 'cms')
	BEGIN
	/**/
		SET @Trem  = N' [MasterObjectId] = ' + CAST(@ObjectId AS NVARCHAR(50))		
	/**/
	END
	/**/
	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues_Lang]  WHERE FieldValueId IN 
			(SELECT FormFieldValueId 
			 FROM dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
			 WHERE MasterValueId IN(
									SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
									FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues] 
									WHERE ' + @Trem +
									' AND [FormId] IN (
											SELECT [FormId]
											FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
											WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))
											OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
											)
									) ) /**/ '

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldValues] 
						WHERE MasterValueId IN(
								SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
								WHERE ' + @Trem +
								' AND [FormId] IN (
										SELECT [FormId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
										WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))
										OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
										)
							) /**/ '

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE dbo.[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValueDetails] 
						WHERE MasterValueId IN(
								SELECT [FormFieldMasterValueId]
								FROM [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
								WHERE ' + @Trem +
								' AND [FormId] IN (
										SELECT [FormId]
										FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
										WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))
										OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
										)
							)/**/ '

	SET @Qry = @Qry + N'DELETE [dbo].[CrmDyFormFieldMasterValues]
						WHERE ' + @Trem +
						' AND [FormId] IN (
						SELECT [FormId]
						FROM [dbo].[CrmDyForms]
						WHERE [CrmSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))
						OR [FOSysPartId] IN (SELECT [SystemPartId] FROM [dbo].[CrmSystemParts] WHERE LOWER([Part]) = LOWER('''+@SysPart+'''))  
					)'
	/**/
	EXECUTE(@Qry)
/**/
END







GO

/*AI990516*/
/*RZ990519- Modify this sp*/
Create PROCEDURE [dbo].[ApispMainSearch]
/* 
	EXEC ApispMainSearch '', 3,1, 1033 , 1065
*/
(
@Val NVARCHAR(200),
@UserID INT = NULL,
@SearchType INT,
@LanguageID INT = NULL,
@DefaultLanguageID INT = NULL
)
AS
BEGIN
	/**/
	/*=====================================================*/
	DECLARE @clmn VARCHAR(max), @from VARCHAR(max), @Term VARCHAR(max)
	DECLARE @cmpDataPermision VARCHAR(max)
	IF(@LanguageID IS NULL OR @LanguageID = @DefaultLanguageID)
	BEGIN
		/**/
		IF(ISNULL(@UserID,0) <> -10) /*RZ990519- Modify this sp, userId=-10 => site*/
			EXEC CRMspSetcmpDataPermission @UserID 
		/*--*/
		IF @SearchType = 1 /*--Company & Person*/
		BEGIN 
			/**/
			SET @clmn = 
				'SELECT DISTINCT TOP 500
					C.CompanyID AS ApiCompanyID, 
					C.cmpName AS ApiCmpName, 
					IsNull((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''')+
					 '' '' +IsNull(C.cmpName, '''') AS ApiCmpNameWithTitle, 
					C.cmpNumber AS ApiCmpNumber,
					(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId) AS ApiCmpTitle, 
					C.cmpFame AS ApiCmpFame,
					(SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups WHERE CompanyGroupID = C.cmpGroupId) AS ApiCmpGroup, 
					(SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups WHERE CompanySubGroupID = C.cmpSubGroupId) AS ApiCmpSubGroup,  
					(SELECT GroupIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII WHERE CompanyGroupIIID = C.cmpGroupIdII) AS ApiCmpGroupII, 
					(SELECT CompanyTypeName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyType WHERE CompanyTypeID = C.cmpTypeId) AS ApiCmpType, 
					(SELECT Presentation FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPresentation WHERE CompanyPresentationID = C.cmpPresentationId) AS ApiCmpPresentation,
					C.cmpPreTel AS ApiCmpPreTel, 
					C.cmpTel AS ApiCmpTel, 
					C.cmpTelUpTo AS ApiCmpTelUpTo,
					C.cmpPreTelII AS ApiCmpPreTelII, 
					C.cmpTelII AS ApiCmpTelII, 
					C.cmpTelUpToII AS ApiCmpTelUpToII,
					C.cmpMobile AS ApiCmpMobile, 
					C.cmpFax AS ApiCmpFax,	 
					(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country WHERE CountryId = C.cmpCountryId) AS ApiCountry,  
					(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId) AS ApiProvince, 
					(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = C.cmpCityId) AS ApiCity, 	
					C.cmpAddress AS ApiCmpAddress, 
					C.cmpReferUserId AS ApiCmpReferUserId,
					(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId) AS ApiReferUser, 	
					P.prsLName AS ApiPrsLName,
					P.prsFName AS ApiPrsFName,
					ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle PT WHERE PT.PersontitleID = P.prsTitleId), '''') +
					 '' '' + 	ISNULL(P.prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ ISNULL(P.prsFName, '''') AS ApiPrsName, 
					P.prsTel AS ApiPrsTel, 
					P.prsTelDesc AS ApiPrsTelDesc, 
					P.prsTelII AS ApiPrsTelII, 
					P.prsMobile AS ApiPrsMobile, 
					P.prsFax AS ApiPrsFax, 
					'' '' ApiCnvsSubject,
					'' '' ApiCnvsTopic,
					'' '' ApiCnvsDesc,
					( SELECT TOP 1 RTrim(P.PrjTitle + '' '' + ISNULL(prjPostFix, '''')) FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE prjCompanyId = CompanyID AND ISNULL(prjIsFinished, 0) = 0 
					ORDER BY prjOrderView desc, ProjectId desc) AS ApiPrjTitle, 
					P.CompanyPersonId AS ApiPrsId,
					( SELECT TOP 1 ProjectId FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE prjCompanyId = CompanyID AND ISNULL(prjIsFinished, 0) = 0 
					ORDER BY prjOrderView desc, ProjectId desc) AS ApiPrjId,
					0 ApiConversationID,
					P.prsNationalCode AS ApiPrsNationalCode, 
					C.cmpNationalCode AS ApiCmpNationalCode'   
			SET @from =
				' FROM dbo.CrmCompany C 
						LEFT JOIN 
							dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P 
								ON C.CompanyID = P.prsCompanyId
						/*LEFT JOIN 
							dbo.CrmProjects Prj
								ON C.CompanyID = Prj.prjCompanyId*/		
				WHERE 1=1 
				AND	P.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
					(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23) 
			' 
			/*--*/		
			SET @Term = ' AND (REPLACE(CAST(C.cmpName as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpNumber LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpPreTel LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpPreTelII LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+   
				   ' OR C.cmpTel LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpTelII LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpFax LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpMobile LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+   
				   ' OR REPLACE(CAST(C.cmpaddress as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR REPLACE(CAST(P.prsLName as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR REPLACE(CAST(P.prsFName as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR P.prsTel LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR P.prsTelII LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR P.prsMobile LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR P.prsFax LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%''' + 
				   ' OR P.prsNationalCode LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.CmpCode LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+	   
					' OR C.cmpNationalCode LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+

				   ')'	
			/**/   
		END
		ELSE
		IF @SearchType = 2 /*--Conversation*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @clmn = 
				 ' SELECT DISTINCT TOP 500  
					C.CompanyID AS ApiCompanyID, 
					C.cmpName AS ApiCmpName, 
					IsNull((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId), '''')+ 
					'' '' +IsNull(C.cmpName, '''') AS ApiCmpNameWithTitle, 
					C.cmpNumber AS ApiCmpNumber,
					(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId) AS ApiCmpTitle, 
					C.cmpFame AS ApiCmpFame,
					(SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups WHERE CompanyGroupID = C.cmpGroupId) AS ApiCmpGroup, 
					(SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups WHERE CompanySubGroupID = C.cmpSubGroupId) AS ApiCmpSubGroup, 
					(SELECT GroupIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII WHERE CompanyGroupIIID = C.cmpGroupIdII) AS ApiCmpGroupII, 
					(SELECT CompanyTypeName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyType WHERE CompanyTypeID = C.cmpTypeId) AS ApiCmpType, 
					(SELECT Presentation FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPresentation WHERE CompanyPresentationID = C.cmpPresentationId) AS ApiCmpPresentation,
					C.cmpPreTel AS ApiCmpPreTel, 
					C.cmpTel AS ApiCmpTel, 
					C.cmpTelUpTo AS ApiCmpTelUpTo,
					C.cmpPreTelII AS ApiCmpPreTelII, 
					C.cmpTelII AS ApiCmpTelII, 
					C.cmpTelUpToII AS ApiCmpTelUpToII,
					C.cmpMobile AS ApiCmpMobile, 
					C.cmpFax AS ApiCmpFax,	 
					(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country WHERE CountryId = C.cmpCountryId) AS ApiCountry, 
					(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId) AS ApiProvince,  
					(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = C.cmpCityId) AS ApiCity, 	
					C.cmpAddress AS ApiCmpAddress, 
					C.cmpReferUserId AS ApiCmpReferUserId,	
					(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId =  C.cmpReferUserId) AS ApiReferUser, 
					P.prsLName AS ApiPrsLName,
					P.prsFName AS ApiPrsFName,
					ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle PT WHERE PT.PersontitleID = P.prsTitleId), '''')+
					 '' '' + ISNULL(P.prsLName, '''') + '' ''+ ISNULL(P.prsFName, '''') AS ApiPrsName, 
					P.prsTel AS ApiPrsTel, 
					P.prsTelDesc AS ApiPrsTelDesc, 
					P.prsTelII AS ApiPrsTelII, 
					P.prsMobile AS ApiPrsMobile, 
					P.prsFax AS ApiPrsFax, 
					Cn.cnvsSubject AS ApiCnvsSubject,
					(SELECT Topic FROM dbo.CrmConversationTopics WHERE ConversationTopicsID = Cn.cnvsTopicId) AS ApiCnvsTopic,
					'' '' ApiCnvsDesc,
					RTrim(Prj.PrjTitle + '' '' + IsNull(prjPostFix, '''')) AS ApiPrjTitle,
					P.CompanyPersonId AS ApiPrsId,
					prj.ProjectId AS ApiPrjId,
					0 ApiConversationID,
					P.prsNationalCode AS ApiPrsNationalCode,
					C.cmpNationalCode AS ApiCmpNationalCode' 
			SET @from = 
				' From dbo.CrmCompany C 
						LEFT JOIN 
							dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P 
								ON C.CompanyID = P.prsCompanyId
						LEFT JOIN 
							dbo.CrmProjects Prj
								ON C.CompanyID = Prj.prjCompanyId					
						LEFT JOIN 
							dbo.CrmConversation Cn
								ON C.CompanyID = Cn.cnvsCompanyId		
				WHERE 1=1 
				AND	P.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
					(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION

					(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION

					(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
						SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))				
				)
				'

			SET @Term = '
				   And (REPLACE(CAST(C.cmpName as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpNumber LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpPreTel LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpPreTelII LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+   
				   ' OR C.cmpTel LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpTelII LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpFax LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpMobile LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+   
				   ' OR REPLACE(CAST(C.cmpaddress as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR REPLACE(CAST(P.prsLName as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR REPLACE(CAST(P.prsFName as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR P.prsTel LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR P.prsTelII LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR P.prsMobile LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR P.prsFax LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+

				   ' OR (SELECT Topic FROM dbo.CrmConversationTopics WHERE ConversationTopicsID = Cn.cnvsTopicId) LIKE ''%' + 

					@Val  +'%'''+ 
				   ' OR P.prsNationalCode LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.CmpCode LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
				   ' OR C.cmpNationalCode LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
					')'
				/*--*/
		END
		ELSE
		IF @SearchType = 3 /*--CnvsDesc*/

					( SELECT cmpTitleId FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId)

					ISNULL( ( SELECT cmpName FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId)
					, '''') AS ApiCmpNameWithTitle, 		
					( SELECT cmpNumber FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpNumber,		
					(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleID = C.cmpTitleId) AS ApiCmpTitle, 
					( SELECT cmpFame FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpFame,

					(SELECT GroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroups WHERE CompanyGroupID = 
					( SELECT cmpGroupId FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId)	
					) AS ApiCmpGroup, 
					(SELECT SubGroupName FROM dbo.CrmCompanySubGroups WHERE CompanySubGroupID = 
					( SELECT cmpSubGroupId FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId)	
					) AS ApiCmpSubGroup, 
					(SELECT GroupIIName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyGroupsII WHERE CompanyGroupIIID = 
					( SELECT cmpGroupIdII FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId)	
					) AS ApiCmpGroupII, 
					(SELECT CompanyTypeName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyType WHERE CompanyTypeID = 
					( SELECT cmpTypeId FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId)	
					) AS ApiCmpType,  
					(SELECT Presentation FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPresentation WHERE CompanyPresentationID = 
					( SELECT cmpPresentationId FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId)	
					) AS ApiCmpPresentation,
					( SELECT cmpPreTel FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpPreTel, 
					( SELECT cmpTel FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpTel, 
					( SELECT cmpTelUpTo FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpTelUpTo,
					( SELECT cmpTelUpTo FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpPreTelII, 
					( SELECT cmpTelUpTo FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpTelII, 
					( SELECT cmpTelUpToII FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpTelUpToII,
					( SELECT cmpMobile FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpMobile, 
					( SELECT cmpFax FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpFax,
					(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country WHERE CountryId = 
					( SELECT cmpCountryId FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId)
					) AS ApiCountry,  
					(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province WHERE ProvinceId = 
					( SELECT cmpProvinceId FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId)
					) AS ApiProvince,  
					(SELECT City FROM dbo.City WHERE CityId = 
					( SELECT cmpCityId FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId)
					) AS ApiCity, 			
					( SELECT cmpAddress FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpAddress, 
					( SELECT cmpReferUserId FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) AS ApiCmpReferUserId, 
					(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''')
					FROM dbo.Users WHERE UserId =  
								( SELECT cmpReferUserId FROM CrmCompany cc WHERE cc.CompanyId = cn.cnvscompanyId) 
					) AS ApiReferUser, 	
					(SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cp WHERE cp.CompanyPersonID = cn.cnvsPersonId) AS ApiPrsLName,
					(SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cp WHERE cp.CompanyPersonID = cn.cnvsPersonId) AS ApiPrsFName,
					ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle PT WHERE PT.PersontitleID = 
					(SELECT prsTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cp WHERE cp.CompanyPersonID = cn.cnvsPersonId)
					), '''') +	 '' '' + 
					ISNULL(
					(SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cp WHERE cp.CompanyPersonID = cn.cnvsPersonId)
					, '''') + '' ''+ 
					ISNULL(
					(SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cp WHERE cp.CompanyPersonID = cn.cnvsPersonId)
					, '''') AS ApiPrsName, 
					(SELECT prsTel FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cp WHERE cp.CompanyPersonID = cn.cnvsPersonId) AS ApiPrsTel, 
					(SELECT prsTelDesc FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cp WHERE cp.CompanyPersonID = cn.cnvsPersonId) AS ApiPrsTelDesc, 
					(SELECT prsTelII FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cp WHERE cp.CompanyPersonID = cn.cnvsPersonId) AS ApiPrsTelII, 
					(SELECT prsMobile FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cp WHERE cp.CompanyPersonID = cn.cnvsPersonId) AS ApiPrsMobile, 
					(SELECT prsFax FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons cp WHERE cp.CompanyPersonID = cn.cnvsPersonId) AS ApiPrsFax, 
					Cn.cnvsSubject AS ApiCnvsSubject,
					(SELECT Topic FROM dbo.CrmConversationTopics WHERE ConversationTopicsID = Cn.cnvsTopicId) AS ApiCnvsTopic,
					''''  ApiCnvsDesc,
					( SELECT TOP 1 RTrim(P.PrjTitle + '' '' + ISNULL(prjPostFix, '''')) FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE prjCompanyId = CompanyID
					AND ISNULL(prjIsFinished, 0) = 0   
					ORDER BY prjOrderView DESC, ProjectId DESC) AS ApiPrjTitle,
					cn.cnvsPersonId AS ApiPrsId,
					( SELECT TOP 1 ProjectId FROM dbo.CrmProjects P WHERE prjCompanyId = CompanyID  
					AND ISNULL(prjIsFinished, 0) = 0 
					ORDER BY prjOrderView DESC, ProjectId DESC) AS ApiPrjId,  
					Cn.ConversationID AS ApiConversationID ,
					NULL ApiPrsNationalCode ,
					C.cmpNationalCode AS ApiCmpNationalCode' 
			SET @from = 		
				' From dbo.CrmCompany C 
						LEFT JOIN 
							/*dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P

				(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang WHERE UserId = C.cmpReferUserId AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR

		(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS ApiReferUser, 	
				(SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + ') AS ApiPrsLName,
				(SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + ') AS ApiPrsFName,
				ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PT WHERE PT.PersontitleID = P.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + '), '''') +'' '' + 	ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND LanguageID = ' + 

		CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + '), '''') + '' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND 

		LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + '), '''') AS ApiPrsName, 
				P.prsTel AS ApiPrsTel, 
				(SELECT prsTelDesc FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + ') AS ApiPrsTelDesc,
				P.prsTelII AS ApiPrsTelII, 
				P.prsMobile AS ApiPrsMobile, 
				P.prsFax AS ApiPrsFax,
				(SELECT cnvsSubject FROM dbo.CrmConversation_Lang WHERE ConversationID = Cn.ConversationID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + ') AS ApiCnvsSubject, 
				(SELECT Topic FROM dbo.CrmConversationTopics_Lang WHERE ConversationTopicsID = Cn.cnvsTopicId AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + ') AS ApiCnvsTopic,
				'' '' ApiCnvsDesc,
				(SELECT RTrim(PrjTitle + '' '' + IsNull(prjPostFix, ''''))
				 FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang WHERE ProjectID = Prj.ProjectID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS ApiPrjTitle, 
				P.CompanyPersonId AS ApiPrsId,
				prj.ProjectId AS ApiPrjId,
				0 ApiConversationID ,
				P.prsNationalCode AS ApiPrsNationalCode,
				C.cmpNationalCode AS ApiCmpNationalCode'
		SET @from = 
			' From dbo.CrmCompany C 
					LEFT JOIN 
						dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P 
							ON C.CompanyID = P.prsCompanyId
					LEFT JOIN 
						dbo.CrmProjects Prj
							ON C.CompanyID = Prj.prjCompanyId					
					LEFT JOIN 
						dbo.CrmConversation Cn
							ON C.CompanyID = Cn.cnvsCompanyId		
			Where 1=1 
			AND	P.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
				(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
				SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)
			AND	Prj.[prjStarterUserId] IN 
					(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 24)
			AND (
				(Cn.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
				(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))
				OR (Cn.[cnvsUserId] IN 
				(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))
				OR (Cn.[cnvsReferUserId] IN 
				(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 25))				
			)' 
		/*--*/		
		SET @Term = '
				AND (REPLACE(CAST((SELECT cmpName FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang WHERE CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) +

			   ' OR C.cmpNumber LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
			   ' OR C.cmpPreTel LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+

			   ' OR REPLACE(CAST((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR

		(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
			   ' OR REPLACE(CAST((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR

		(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS NVARCHAR(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+

			   ' OR P.prsTelII LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+

			   ' OR (SELECT Topic FROM dbo.CrmConversationTopics_Lang WHERE ConversationTopicsID = Cn.cnvsTopicId AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + ') LIKE ''%' + 
	        
		@Val  +'%'''+
			   ' OR P.prsNationalCode LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
			   ' OR C.CmpCode LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
			   ' OR C.cmpNationalCode LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+

				Where prjCompanyId = CompanyID AND

				C.cmpTelUpTo AS ApiCmpTelUpTo,
				C.cmpPreTelII AS ApiCmpPreTelII, 
				C.cmpTelII AS ApiCmpTelII, 
				C.cmpTelUpToII AS ApiCmpTelUpToII,
				C.cmpMobile AS ApiCmpMobile, 
				C.cmpFax AS ApiCmpFax,	 
				(SELECT Country FROM dbo.Country_Lang WHERE CountryId = C.cmpCountryId AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS ApiCountry,  
				(SELECT Province FROM dbo.Province_Lang WHERE ProvinceId = C.cmpProvinceId AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + ') 

		AS ApiProvince, 
				(SELECT City FROM dbo.City_Lang WHERE CityId = C.cmpCityId AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS ApiCity, 	
				(SELECT cmpAddress FROM dbo.CrmCompany_Lang WHERE CompanyID = C.CompanyID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + ') 

		AS ApiCmpAddress, 
				C.cmpReferUserId AS ApiCmpReferUserId,	
				(SELECT ISNULL(LName, '''')+'' ''+ISNULL(FName, '''') FROM dbo.Users_Lang WHERE UserId = C.cmpReferUserId AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR

		(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS ApiReferUser, 	
				(SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + ') AS ApiPrsLName,
				(SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + ') AS ApiPrsFName,
				ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle_Lang PT WHERE PT.PersontitleID = P.prsTitleId AND PT.LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + '), '''') +'' '' + 	ISNULL((SELECT prsLName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND LanguageID = ' + 

		CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + '), '''') + '' ''+ ISNULL((SELECT prsFName FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND 

		LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + '), '''') AS ApiPrsName, 
				P.prsTel AS ApiPrsTel, 
				(SELECT prsTelDesc FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons_Lang Where CompanyPersonID = P.CompanyPersonID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), 

		@LanguageID) + ') AS ApiPrsTelDesc,
				P.prsTelII AS ApiPrsTelII, 
				P.prsMobile AS ApiPrsMobile, 
				P.prsFax AS ApiPrsFax, 
				'' '' ApiCnvsSubject,
				'' '' ApiCnvsTopic,
				'' '' ApiCnvsDesc,
				(SELECT RTrim(PrjTitle + '' '' + IsNull(prjPostFix, '''')) 
				 FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang WHERE ProjectID = Prj.ProjectID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + ') AS ApiPrjTitle, 
				P.CompanyPersonId AS ApiPrsId,
				prj.ProjectId AS ApiPrjId, 
				0 ApiConversationID ,
				P.prsNationalCode AS ApiPrsNationalCode,
				C.cmpNationalCode AS ApiCmpNationalCode'
		SET @from = 
			' From dbo.CrmCompany C 
					INNER JOIN dbo.CrmCompanyPersons P
							ON C.CompanyID = P.prsCompanyId
					INNER JOIN dbo.CrmProjects Prj 
							ON C.CompanyID = Prj.prjCompanyId		
			Where 1=1 
			/*RZ990504- Add linkedUser*/
			AND	P.[FirstRegisterUserId] IN 
				(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
				SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 23)
			AND	Prj.[prjStarterUserId] IN 
					(SELECT ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' UNION 
					SELECT LinkedUserId FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers WHERE CrmUserId = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @UserID) + ' AND LinkedTypeId = 24)
			/*RZ990504- End*/ ' 
		/*--*/		
		SET @Term = '
			And ( REPLACE(CAST((SELECT PrjTitle FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang WHERE ProjectID = Prj.ProjectID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + 
		') as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+
		/*ADBF980820- Add search prjPostFix*/
			   ' OR REPLACE(CAST((SELECT prjPostFix FROM dbo.CrmProjects_Lang WHERE ProjectID = Prj.ProjectID AND LanguageID = ' + CONVERT(VARCHAR(10), @LanguageID) + 
		') as nvarchar(2000)) , NCHAR(1740), NCHAR(1610)) LIKE ''%' + @Val  +'%'''+

		')'
		END
		/*--*/
		/*--*/
		IF(ISNULL(@UserID,0) <> -10) /*RZ990519- Modify this sp, userId=-10 => site*/		
		BEGIN
		/**/
			SELECT @cmpDataPermision = TXT
			FROM dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
			WHERE TypeId = 20
			AND UserId = @UserId
			/*--*/
			EXEC ( @clmn + @from + @Term + @cmpDataPermision + ' ORDER BY ApiCompanyId DESC, ApiCmpTitle')
			/*--*/
			DELETE dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo
			WHERE TypeId = 10
			AND UserId = @UserId
			/*--*/
			INSERT INTO dbo.CrmsysDBTempInfo (UserId,	TXT, TypeId)
			 VALUES ( @UserId, ' SELECT CompanyId ' + @from + @Term, 10 ) 
		/**/
		END
		ELSE IF(ISNULL(@UserID,0) = -10) /*RZ990519- Modify this sp, userId=-10 => site*/		
		BEGIN
		/**/
			EXEC ( @clmn + @from + @Term + ' ORDER BY ApiCompanyId DESC, ApiCmpTitle')
		/**/
		END				
		/**/
	END
	/**/
END





GO

		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 32)) /*prjTitleGroup*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@prjSrchCols <> '') SET @prjSrchCols = @prjSrchCols + ','
			SET @prjSrchCols = @prjSrchCols + N'prjTitleGroup'
			/**/
		END
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 33)) /*prjDesc*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@prjSrchCols <> '') SET @prjSrchCols = @prjSrchCols + ','
			SET @prjSrchCols = @prjSrchCols + N'prjDesc'
			/**/
		END
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 34)) /*prjDescI*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@prjSrchCols <> '') SET @prjSrchCols = @prjSrchCols + ','
			SET @prjSrchCols = @prjSrchCols + N'prjDescI'
			/**/
		END

		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 23)) /*cnvsSubject*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@cnvsSrchCols <> '') SET @cnvsSrchCols = @cnvsSrchCols + ','
			SET @cnvsSrchCols = @cnvsSrchCols + N'cnvsSubject'
			/**/
		END
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 24)) /*cnvsTopic*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (ISNULL(@cnvsSrchConditions, '') <> '') SET @cnvsSrchConditions = @cnvsSrchConditions + ' OR '
			SET @cnvsSrchConditions = @cnvsSrchConditions + ' ISNULL((SELECT ISNULL(Topic, '''') FROM CrmConversationTopics WHERE ConversationTopicsID = Cn.cnvsTopicId AND Topic LIKE ''' + @Val + '''), '''') <> '''' '
			/**/
		END
		IF(EXISTS(SELECT TOP(1) CAST([value] AS INT) FROM STRING_SPLIT(@SearchConditionMode, ',') WHERE CAST([value] AS INT) = 25)) /*cnvsDesc*/
		BEGIN
			/**/
			IF (@cnvsSrchCols <> '') SET @cnvsSrchCols = @cnvsSrchCols + ','
			SET @cnvsSrchCols = @cnvsSrchCols + N'cnvsDesc'
			/**/
		END

		/**/
	END
	ELSE  /*IF(ISNULL(@SearchConditionMode,'') = '')*/
	BEGIN

		IF (@SearchType = 1) /*Company & Person*/

			/**/
		END

		END

			/**/
		END
		/**/
	END


	IF (@SearchType = 1) /*Company & Person*/
	BEGIN

				WHERE 1=1 ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '')

			SET @QRY = @QRY + ' ORDER BY FT.[RANK] DESC, CompanyID'
			/**/
		END
		/**/
		IF (LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(@prsSrchCols, ''))) <> '')
		BEGIN
			/**/
			SET @QRY = @QRY + N'
				INSERT INTO @TBL(CmpId, PrsId, rnk, PrsFirstRegUserId)

					P.prsCompanyId,
					P.CompanyPersonID,
					FT.[rank],
					P.FirstRegisterUserId

				INNER JOIN CONTAINSTABLE([CRMvwSelectCompanyPersons' + @ViewsLang + '], (' + @prsSrchCols + '), @VAL' + @FullTextLanguage + ') FT ON P.' + @KeyCrmCompanyPersons + ' = FT.[Key]
				WHERE 1=1 ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '')
				
			SET @QRY = @QRY + 
				' AND ((ISNULL(P.FirstRegisterUserId, ' + CAST(@UserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ') = ' + CAST(@UserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')
					 OR ((P.FirstRegisterUserId <> ' + CAST(@UserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + ')
						AND
							(SELECT COUNT (*)
							FROM dbo.CrmLinkedUsers L
							WHERE L.LinkedTypeId = 23
							AND L.CrmUserId = ' + CAST(@UserID AS NVARCHAR(50)) + '
							AND L.LinkedUserId = P.FirstRegisterUserId ) > 0)) '

		END

	END
	ELSE IF (@SearchType = 3) /*CnvsDesc*/

					(Cn.[FirstRegisterUserId] = ' + CAST(@UserID AS VARCHAR(10)) + '

										OR L.LinkedUserId = Cn.[cnvsReferUserId]

							) > 0
						)

									AND L.LinkedUserId = Prj.[prjStarterUserId]) > 0

						 OR (ISNULL(Prj.[prjStarterUserId],0) = 0) /*RZ14020224- Add order by*/

			(SELECT ISNULL(U.FName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(U.LName, '''') FROM Users' + @ViewsLang + ' U WHERE U.UserId = C.cmpSuppUserId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + ') AS cmpSuppUser,

					ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle' + @ViewsLang + ' P WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId ' + ISNULL(' AND ' + @ViewsLangWhere, '') + '), '''') + '' ''+

				SELECT TOP 1 
					RTrim(P.PrjTitle + '' '' + IsNull(prjPostFix, ''''))

-- [dbo].[CRMfnEmailLoadRptCmpFilterEmailAddressStr] | References permission/temp-filter machinery





/*ESP950924*/
/*RZ14000624- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
Create FUNCTION [dbo].[CRMfnEmailLoadRptCmpFilterEmailAddressStr]
(	
/* 
Select dbo.CRMfnEmailLoadRptCmpFilterEmailAddressStr(3)
*/
@UserId INT
)
RETURNS VARCHAR(max) /*ESP950924*/
AS 
BEGIN
/*--DECLARE @Mode INT
--SET @Mode = 2
--*/
DECLARE @String VARCHAR(max) /*ESP950924*/
SET @String =
' 
Select 
	T.CompanyID,
	T.CompanyPersonID,
	T.CompanyName,
	PersonName,
	Mobile,
	LEN(Mobile) MobileLength,
	LEFT(Mobile, 2) MobilePreNum,
	Phone,
	cmpTitle,
	cmpName,
	prsTitle,
	prsFName,
	prsLName,
	prsJob
	
From (
SELECT DISTINCT LTRIM(RTRIM(cmpMobile)) Mobile, 
	'''' PersonName, 
	/*LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(c.cmpName, ''''))) CompanyName,*/
	/*RZ14000624- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	LTRIM(RTRIM(IsNULL((CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
		ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
			WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
			)				
	END),'''')))CompanyName,
	/*RZ14000624- End*/

	ISNULL(cmpPreTel, '''') + cmpTel Phone,
	0 CompanyPersonID,
	CompanyID,
	/*(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleId = c.cmpTitleId) cmpTitle,
	cmpNAME cmpName,*/
	/*RZ14000624- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
		ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
			WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
			)				
	END) cmpTitle,
	(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
		ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
			WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
			)				
	END) cmpName,
	/*RZ14000624- End*/
	'''' prsTitle,
	'''' prsFName,
	'''' prsLName,
	'''' prsJob
FROM dbo.CrmCompany c
WHERE cmpEmail <> '' ''
UNION
SELECT DISTINCT LTRIM(RTRIM(prsMobile)) Mobile, 
	LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle CPT WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId), '''') +'' ''+ 
	ISNULL(prsFName, '''') +'' ''+ 
	ISNULL(prsLName, '''') )) PersonName, 
	/*ISNULL(
	(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleId = 
		(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) 
		), '''') + '' ''+
	(SELECT cmpNAME FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) CompanyName,*/

	/*RZ14000624- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	(SELECT 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
						ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
							ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) CompanyName,
	/*RZ14000624- End*/

	ISNULL(prsPreTel, '''')+prsTel Phone,
	CompanyPersonID CompanyPersonID,
	prsCompanyId CompanyID,
	/*(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleId = 
		(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) 	) cmpTitle,
	(SELECT cmpNAME FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) cmpName,*/

	/*RZ14000624- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	(SELECT 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) cmpTitle,
	(SELECT 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
				THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
				ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
					FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
					WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
					)				
			END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) cmpName,
	/*RZ14000624- End*/

	(SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle CPT WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId) prsTitle,
	prsFName,
	prsLName,
	(SELECT Jobs FROM dbo.CrmPersonJobs WHERE prsJobId = PersonJobID) prsJob
FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
WHERE (prsEmail <> '' '' OR prsEmailII <> '' '' )
/*And IsNull(prsStopSendSms, '''') <> 1 */
) T

INNER JOIN 
( 
'
/*--*/
DECLARE @R VARCHAR(max) /*ESP950924*/
SELECT @R=Txt FROM CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
/*--*/
SET @String = @String + 
' SELECT CompanyId, CompanyPersonId FROM (' 
+ 
@R 
+
') TT) ttt ON T.CompanyId = ttt.CompanyId 
		And T.CompanyPersonId = ttt.CompanyPersonId
'
--
--
RETURN((@String))
END

-- [dbo].[CRMfnsmsLoadRptCmpFilterMobileNumberStr] | References permission/temp-filter machinery







/*ESP950924*/
/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
/*RZ14010302- Add cmpUsername and password*/
/*RZ14020802- Check Have cmpPassword*/
Create FUNCTION [dbo].[CRMfnsmsLoadRptCmpFilterMobileNumberStr]
(	
/* 
Select dbo.CRMfnsmsLoadRptCmpFilterMobileNumberStr( 3)
*/
@UserId INT
)
RETURNS VARCHAR(max) /*ESP950924*/
AS 
BEGIN
--DECLARE @Mode INT
--SET @Mode = 2
--
DECLARE @String VARCHAR(max) /*ESP950924*/
SET @String =
' 
Select 
	T.CompanyID,
	T.CompanyPersonID,
	T.CompanyName,
	PersonName,
	Mobile,
	LEN(Mobile) MobileLength,
	LEFT(Mobile, 2) MobilePreNum,
	Phone,
	cmpTitle,
	cmpName,
	prsTitle,
	prsFName,
	prsLName,
	prsJob
	/*RZ14010302- Add cmpUsername and password*/
	,T.[cmpUserName]
    /*,LEFT((SUBSTRING(T.[cmpPassword], 6, Len(T.[cmpPassword]))), ISNULL(Len((SUBSTRING(T.[cmpPassword], 6, Len(T.[cmpPassword])))),0) -4) [cmpPassword]*/
	/*RZ14010302- End*/
	/*RZ14020802- Check Have cmpPassword*/
	,(CASE WHEN ISNULL(T.[cmpPassword],'''') <> ''''
			THEN (LEFT((SUBSTRING(T.[cmpPassword], 6, Len(T.[cmpPassword]))), ISNULL(Len((SUBSTRING(T.[cmpPassword], 6, Len(T.[cmpPassword])))),0) -4))
			ELSE ''''
	 END) [cmpPassword]
	/*RZ14020802- End*/

From (
SELECT DISTINCT LTRIM(RTRIM(cmpMobile)) Mobile, 
	'''' PersonName, 
	/*LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(cmpName, ''''))) CompanyName,*/
	/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		THEN LTRIM(RTRIM((ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))))
		ELSE	(SELECT LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')))
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
			WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
			)				
	END) CompanyName,
	/*RZ14000621- End*/
	ISNULL(cmpPreTel, '''') + cmpTel Phone,
	0 CompanyPersonID,
	CompanyID,
	/*(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleId = cmpTitleId) cmpTitle,
	cmpNAME cmpName,*/
	/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
		ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
			WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
			)				
	END) cmpTitle,
	(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
		THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
		ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
			FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
			WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
			)				
	END) cmpName,
	/*RZ14000621- End*/	
	'''' prsTitle,
	'''' prsFName,
	'''' prsLName,
	'''' prsJob
	/*RZ14010302- Add cmpUsername and password*/
	,C.[cmpUserName]
    ,C.[cmpPassword]
	/*RZ14010302- End*/
FROM dbo.CrmCompany C
WHERE cmpMobile <> '' ''
UNION
SELECT DISTINCT LTRIM(RTRIM(prsMobile)) Mobile, 
	LTRIM(RTRIM(ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle CPT WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId), '''') +'' ''+ 
	ISNULL(prsFName, '''') +'' ''+ 
	ISNULL(prsLName, '''') )) PersonName, 
	/*ISNULL(
	(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleId = 
		(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) 
		), '''') + '' ''+
	(SELECT cmpNAME FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) CompanyName,*/
	/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	(SELECT 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''') + '' '' + 
					ISNULL(C.[cmpName],'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''')  + '' '' +
						ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) CompanyName,
	/*RZ14000621- End*/

	ISNULL(prsPreTel, '''')+prsTel Phone,
	CompanyPersonID CompanyPersonID,
	prsCompanyId CompanyID,
	/*(SELECT CompanyTitle FROM dbo.CrmCompanyTitle WHERE CompanyTitleId = 
		(SELECT cmpTitleId FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) 	) cmpTitle,
	(SELECT cmpNAME FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) cmpName,*/
	/*RZ14000621- Change cmpName based on cmpKind*/
	(SELECT 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN ((ISNULL((SELECT CompanyTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyTitle] CT WHERE CT.[CompanyTitleID] = C.[cmpTitleId]),'''')))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL((SELECT PersonTitle FROM [dbo].[CrmPersonTitle] PT WHERE PT.PersonTitleID = CCP.prsTitleId),'''') 
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) cmpTitle,
	(SELECT 
		(CASE WHEN ISNULL(C.[cmpKind],0) = 0
			THEN (ISNULL(C.[cmpName],''''))
			ELSE	(SELECT ISNULL(prsFName, '''') + '' '' + ISNULL(prsLName,'''')
				FROM [dbo].[CrmCompanyPersons] CCP
				WHERE CCP.CompanyPersonID = C.[cmpMainPersonId]		
				)				
		END)CompanyName				
	FROM  [dbo].[CrmCompany] C
	WHERE C.CompanyID = prsCompanyId) cmpName,
	/*RZ14000621- End*/	
	(SELECT PersonTitle FROM dbo.CrmPersonTitle CPT WHERE PersonTitleId = prsTitleId) prsTitle,
	prsFName,
	prsLName,
	(SELECT Jobs FROM dbo.CrmPersonJobs WHERE prsJobId = PersonJobID) prsJob
	/*RZ14010302- Add cmpUsername and password*/
	,(SELECT [cmpUserName] FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) [cmpUserName]
    ,(SELECT [cmpPassword] FROM dbo.CrmCompany WHERE CompanyId = prsCompanyId) [cmpPassword]
	/*RZ14010302- End*/

FROM dbo.CrmCompanyPersons
WHERE prsMobile <> '' ''
And IsNull(prsStopSendSms, '''') <> 1
) T

INNER JOIN 
( 
'
--
DECLARE @R VARCHAR(max) /*ESP950924*/
SELECT @R=Txt FROM CrmsysDBTempInfo WHERE UserId = @UserId AND TypeId = 1
--
SET @String = @String + 
' SELECT CompanyId, CompanyPersonId FROM (' 
+ 
@R 
+
') TT) ttt ON T.CompanyId = ttt.CompanyId 
		And T.CompanyPersonId = ttt.CompanyPersonId
'
--
--
RETURN((@String))
END
